L1716: ## 7.1 *In Vitro* Reverse Mutation Assay in Bacterial Cells (Ames)
L1717: Study title: **Bacterial Reverse Mutation Test in Salmonella Typhimurium and Eschericia Coli**
L1718: Study no.: 9600210
Study report location: Volume 8, pages 1 to 49
Conducting laboratory and location: [REDACTED] (b) (4)
Date of study initiation: March 7, 2013
GLP compliance: The OECD principle of GLP compliance was followed.
QA statement: Yes
Drug, lot #, and % purity: [REDACTED] (b) (4)
L1719: ## **Key Study Findings**
L1720: [REDACTED] (b) (4) at dose levels tested in the presence or absence of S9 mix did not show any genotoxic activity in the Salmonella typhimurium and Eschericia coli in *in vitro* mutagenicity assay.
L1721: ## Methods
L1722: Strains: TA 1535, TA 1537, TA 98, TA 100, WP2
uvrA
Concentrations in definitive study: [REDACTED] (b) (4)
\mug/ml
Basis of concentration selection: Standard limit dose maximum [REDACTED] (b) (4), and the maximum practical solubility of the test item in the vehicle [REDACTED] (b) (4)
Negative control: Dimethyl sulfoxide (DMSO)
Positive control: [REDACTED] (b) (4)
Vehicle: 0.2 % L-ascorbic acid in water, pH [REDACTED] (b) (4)
Incubation &amp; sampling time: 64 hours and 30 minutes with and without metabolic activation
L1723: ## **Study Validity**
L1724: For study validity, the mean revertant colony counts of the vehicle control for each strain should be close to, or within the current historical control range of the laboratory. All positive controls (with S9 mix where required) should produce increases in revertant colony numbers to at least twice the concurrent vehicle control levels with the appropriate bacterial strain (b)^{(4)}x for TA 100).
L1725: ## **Results**
L1726: Following exposure to [REDACTED] (b)^{(4)} (impurities of ALXN1101), the mean revertant colony counts for the vehicle and the test items were within the laboratory historical control range, and the background lawns were normal.
L1727: The sterility check plate for the vehicle was contaminated (1 colony), but the sterility check plates for the formulations of the test item were not. The contamination was observed only on a few plates but the formulations were sterile and did not obscure the colony counts, nor affect the outcome of the study.
L1728: The appropriate positive controls (with S9 mix where required) induced increases in revertant colony numbers to at least twice the concurrent vehicle levels with the appropriate bacterial strain (b)^{(4)}x for TA 100), confirming the sensitivity of the test system and activity of the S9 mix.
L1729: An invalid result was however obtained when WP2 uvrA was treated with [REDACTED] (b)^{(4)} in the presence of S9. The assay was still considered valid because the sensitivity of WP2 uvrA was confirmed in the absence of S9 using [REDACTED] (b)^{(4)}, while the effectiveness of the S9 mix was confirmed by the positive results for the other bacterial strains in the presence of S9.
L1730: When exposed to [REDACTED] (b)^{(4)}, there were no incomplete or absent background lawns of non-revertant bacteria, and there were no substantial reductions in revertant colony counts. This result indicated that the test items were non-toxic to the bacteria at the levels tested. There was also no precipitation observed in the assay.
L1731: The exposure to [REDACTED] (b)^{(4)} at all dose levels tested in the presence or absence of S9 mix did not result in any increases in revertant colony numbers with any of the tester strains. A summary of the assay results are presented in the sponsor's table below.
L1732: (b) (4)
L1733: ## 7.2 *In Vitro* Assays in Mammalian Cells
L1734: ## Study title: In Vitro Mammalian Chromosome Aberration Test in Human Peripheral Blood Lymphocytes
L1735: Study no.:	964426
Study report location:	Volume 8, pages 1 to 69
Conducting laboratory and location:	(b) (4)
Date of study initiation:	October 23, 2012
GLP compliance:	The OECD principle of GLP compliance was followed.
QA statement:	Yes
Drug, lot #, and % purity:	ALXN1101, lot # 856/cPMP/PD/002 and 93.17 % purity.
L1736: ## Key Study Findings
L1737: ALXN1101 did not induce chromosome damage in human peripheral blood lymphocytes at the concentrations used in this *in vitro* study.
L1738: ## Methods
L1739: Cell line:	Human Peripheral Blood Lymphocytes
Concentrations in definitive study:	0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 2.60, 3.90, 4.0, 5.85, 6.0, 8.0, 8.78, 13.2, 19.8, 27.9, 40.8, 58.8, and 83.3 µg/ml
Basis of concentration selection:	The maximum solubility of the test article in the vehicle and the maximum amount of vehicle that can be added to the test system, or the highest level below the toxic concentration.
Negative control:	Formulation vehicle (A)
Positive control:	[Redacted] (b) (4)
Formulation/Vehicle:	[Redacted] (b) (4) L-Ascorbic Acid (2 mg/ml); Mannitol [Redacted] (b) (4) mg/ml); Water; pH adjusted to [Redacted] (b) (4)
Incubation & sampling time:	4 hr (In the absence and presence of S9) and 21 hr (In the absence of S9 mix)
L1740: ## Study Validity
L1741: Assay validity is based on the vehicle/negative control findings being within or close to the historical control range, while the positive control reference items should produce a substantial increase in the incidence of aberrant cells (at least twice) compared with the concurrent control with values remaining beyond the 99 % upper limit of the historical negative/vehicle control range. Based on these criteria, the study was valid.
L1742: ## Results
L1743: ALXN1101 did not cause any substantial increases in the proportion of aberrant metaphases at any of the experimental points as shown in the summary result table below. The proportion of aberrant metaphases for all the vehicle control and test item groups was within the laboratory negative historical control range as shown in the historical control values figure below.
L1744: There were also no substantial increases in the incidence of chromatid or chromosome gaps or polyploidy observed at any experimental point. No precipitation or change in culture medium color was observed.
L1745: However, the appearance of a ring-like structure between chromatids was observed during the 21 hour incubation period at the highest dose level (83.3 µg/ml) tested. The ring-like structure was not classified as a chromosomal aberration and the biological significance of the finding is unknown. Summary tables of study design and assay results from the sponsor's submission are presented below.

L1794: ## 10 Special Toxicology Studies
L1795: ## **ALXN1101: An *In Vitro* Hemolysis Assay with Human Whole Blood (Study # 2087-004)**
L1796: **Methods:** The objective of this study was to evaluate the potential of a range of concentrations of ALXN1101 to hemolyze human red blood cells *in vitro*. Pooled blood was collected from a fasting non-smoker donor with no known medication or recreational drug use, and 32 aliquot vials of 0.5 ml of whole blood samples were labeled as Blood A, B, C, and D, consisting of 4 groups of 8 vials each. The appropriate volume of negative control (0.9 % NaCl), test article (ALXN1101), positive control (sterile water), and vehicle (ALXN1101 IV vehicle) were mixed with Blood A thru D vials as a (v/v) ratio, respectively. The concentrations of the test article added to Blood vials B were 0.25, 0.14, 0.08, and 0.045 mg/ml, at a dilution ratio of 1:1, 1:2.5, 1:5, and 1:10, respectively. From the separated plasma, 0.5 ml each of plasma was also aliquoted into 3 vials labeled Plasma A, B, and C, and the plasma visually inspected for hemolysis. The appropriate volume of the negative control (0.9 % NaCl), test article (0.25 mg/ml ALXN1101), and vehicle was added as a ratio to the plasma (v/v) of 1:1 and was added to the plasma vials A, B, and C, respectively. All the blood and plasma vials were gently mixed, and incubated at 37 °C for 10 min. Following incubation, the plasma samples were visually inspected for turbidity whereas plasma free hemoglobin levels in the blood samples were measured.
L1797: ## Results
L1798: The incubation of plasma with saline, vehicle, or formulated ALXN1101 (0.25 mg/ml) at a 1:1 dilution did not produce any turbidity or precipitation.
L1799: The incubation of red blood cells with vehicle or formulated ALXN1101 (0.045 to 0.25 mg/ml) did not increase the concentration of free hemoglobin, when compared to the saline (negative controls) and therefore did not produce any appreciable hemolysis of red blood cells. The positive control (sterile water), produced an increase in the concentration of free hemoglobin, hence hemolysis of red blood cells. The findings are summarized in the sponsor's tables below.

L1808: ## 11 Integrated Summary and Safety Evaluation
L1809: ALXN1101 is a synthetic pterin derivative known as cPMP, formulated as a powder for reconstitution for IV use in the treatment of molybdenum cofactor deficiency (MoCD). MoCD is a rare, severe, neonatal-onset disorder with autosomal recessive inheritance caused by defects in the biosynthesis of molybdenum cofactor (MoCo). MoCD type A affects two thirds (66 %) of MoCD patients and is caused by a mutation in the MOCS1 gene. The sponsor proposed to use ALXN1101 as a substitute in the MoCo biosynthesis pathway to restore SO level and reduce the levels of sulfite and SSC, thereby alleviating the MoCD disease severity.
L1810: The sponsor proposed a randomized, blinded, placebo-controlled, single-dose, sequential-cohort, dose-escalation first-in-human (FIH) study to evaluate the safety, tolerability, and pharmacokinetics (PK) of a single intravenous (IV) dose of ALXN1101 in healthy adult male and female subjects. Thirty two study subjects between the ages of 18 and 60 years and weighing between 55 and 100 kg will be randomized into 4 dose cohorts of 0.10, 0.32, 0.90, and 1.50 mg/kg. Each dose cohort will consist of 8 subject cohorts of six active drug subjects and 2 placebo subjects.
L1811: The sponsor submitted preclinical pharmacology, safety pharmacology, pharmacokinetic, genotoxicity and toxicology studies in support of the IND.
L1812: The effectiveness of ALXN1101 was demonstrated in an in vitro assay in which it caused an increase in molybdopterin (MPT) synthesis and functional restoration of sulfite oxidase (SO) activity.
L1813: The pharmacodynamic effect of ALXN1101 was also demonstrated *in vivo* in a mouse model of molybdenum cofactor deficiency (MoCD) where 2 – 4 µg of rcPMP or ALXN1101, administered 3 times/week effectively rescued knockout MOC-/- mice pups from premature death.
L1814: In safety pharmacology studies, daily intravenous administration of ALXN1101 at dose levels of 1 - 10 mg/kg/day to Sprague Dawley rats for 14 days did not adversely affect neurobehavioral and pulmonary functions. Similarly, administration of ALXN1101 at 1-10 mg/kg/day in Beagle dogs for 7 days did not affect cardiovascular functions.
L1815: ALXN1101, 0, 1, 3, or 10 mg/kg, was administered for 14 days to Sprague Dawley rats and Beagle dogs, followed by a 4-week recovery period to study its pharmacokinetics and metabolism of the drug.
L1816: There was no accumulation of ALXN1101 at any of the tested doses in either species, and there was no difference in the exposure between males and females at any tested dose in either species. The mean ALXN1101 C_{max} values in male and female rats ranged from 220 \pm 14.9 ng/ml to 2720 \pm 300 ng/ml on day 1 and from 233 \pm 7.07 ng/ml to 2660 \pm 209 ng/ml on day 14, and the mean exposure (AUC_{0-3}) level ranged from 339 to 4100 ng\cdoth/ml on day 1 and from 349 to 3890 ng\cdoth/ml on day 14 in male and female rats. The mean ALXN1101 C_{max} values ranged from 613 \pm 49.9 ng/ml, to 5898 \pm 1040 ng/ml on day 1 and from 660 \pm 43 ng/ml, to 6352 \pm 1260 ng/ml on day 14 in male and female dogs; and the mean exposure (AUC_{0-3}) level ranged from 1023 \pm 96.9 ng\cdoth/ml to 9794 \pm 1630 ng\cdoth/ml on day 1 and from 1066 \pm 79.9 ng\cdoth/ml to 10,402 \pm 2160 ng\cdoth/ml on day 14 in male and female dogs.
L1817: In rats, the estimated terminal half-lives could not be accurately estimated in the 1 or 3 mg/kg dose group, but were 0.603 h and 0.706 h in the 10 mg/kg dose group on days 1 and 14, respectively.
L1818: In dogs, the estimated terminal half-lives on day 1 were 0.726 h, 0.638 h, and 0.820 h in 1, 3, and 10 mg/kg dose groups, respectively. On day 14, the estimated half-lives were 0.723 h and 0.756 h in the 3 and 10 mg/kg dose groups, respectively, and could not be estimated in the 1 mg/kg dose group.
L1819: Five metabolites of ALXN1101 were identified in all the species (mouse, rat, dog and human) studies, without any species-related differences.
L1820: ALXN1101 (0.00271 to 25 µM) was not an inhibitor of human CYP450 isozymes or was not an inducer of CYP450 isozymes.
L1821: ALXN1101 only modestly binds to rat and human erythrocytes as shown by a blood-to-plasma ratio of 0.791 in rats and 0.695 in humans.
L1822: ALXN1101 was studied in repeat-dose (1 to 10 mg/kg/day) toxicology studies in Sprague-Dawley rats and Beagle dogs following repeated dosing for 14 days with a 4-
L1823: week recovery period. ALXN1101 did not show any adverse effects in rats and dogs, and the NOAEL dose was determined to be 10 mg/kg/day in both rats and dogs.
L1824: No evidence of genotoxicity was observed with ALXN1101 in the *in vitro* Ames test, *in vitro* mammalian chromosomal aberration test, or *in vivo* mammalian erythrocyte micronucleus test. There were also no evidence of genotoxicity with the major impurities of ALXN1101 (b) (4) in the Ames test or the chromosomal aberration test.
L1825: In an *in vitro* hemolysis test, ALXN1101 at a concentration range of 0.045 to 0.25 mg/ml did not show any hemolytic activity, precipitation or turbidity following incubation with human blood.
L1826: ## **Recommendation:**
L1827: It is safe to proceed with the proposed clinical dose of ALXN1101 in the study subjects.

Body weight of the mice was measured throughout the study and a retrospective calculation was undertaken to factor in the changing body weight of mice. HED was then calculated by dividing the mouse dose (in mg/kg) by 12.3, which is the human allometric scaling factor recommended by the FDA.

1.1 mg/kg/MOC51^{-/-} vs 4.4 mg/kg/MOC51^{-/-}: P > 0.05
2.2 mg/kg/MOC51^{-/-} vs 4.4 mg/kg/MOC51^{-/-}: P > 0.05
The plot indicates that treatment with 0.1 mg/kg ALXN1101 significantly increases plasma SSC levels in MOC51^{-/-} mice compared to higher doses (1.1, 2.2, 4.4 mg/kg) and control groups (MOC51^{+/+}).
L1881: Figure 9 is a scatter plot showing Urine Uric Acid levels (mg/dL) in MOC51 KO mice at Postnatal Day 9 (PND 9). The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 100 mg/dL. The X-axis shows different treatment groups: Placebo/Untreated MOC51^{-/-} (n=6), 0.1mg/kg (n=5), 1.1mg/kg (n=1), 2.2mg/kg (n=2), 4.4mg/kg (n=4), MOC51^{+/+} (n=16), and MOC51^{-/-} (n=14). Statistical comparisons are indicated by horizontal lines above the data points: p>0.05 for comparisons between Placebo/Untreated MOC51^{-/-} and 0.1mg/kg, 1.1mg/kg, 2.2mg/kg, and 4.4mg/kg groups. p>0.05 is also indicated for comparisons between 0.1mg/kg and 1.1mg/kg, and 2.2mg/kg and 4.4mg/kg groups.
L1882: Figure 10 is a scatter plot showing Urine Uric Acid levels (mg/dL) in MOC51 KO mice at Postnatal Day 28 (PND 28). The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 400 mg/dL. The X-axis shows different treatment groups: 0.1mg/kg AL.XN1101 (n=5), 1.1mg/kg AL.XN1101 (n=7), 2.2mg/kg AL.XN1101 (n=9), 4.4mg/kg AL.XN1101 (n=6), MOC51^{+/+} (n=11), and MOC51^{-/-} (n=14). Statistical comparisons are indicated by horizontal lines above the data points: P<0.05 for comparisons between 0.1mg/kg AL.XN1101 and 1.1mg/kg AL.XN1101, and 2.2mg/kg AL.XN1101 and 4.4mg/kg AL.XN1101 groups. P>0.05 is indicated for comparisons between 1.1mg/kg AL.XN1101 and 2.2mg/kg AL.XN1101, and 4.4mg/kg AL.XN1101 and MOC51^{+/+} groups.
L1883: Figure showing four panels (A, B, C, D) related to the in vitro biological activity of ALXN1101 and E. coli derived cPMP. Panels A and B are HPLC chromatograms showing fluorescence (LU) versus retention time (min) for MPT synthesis, comparing different concentrations of ALXN1101 (1 HBr). Panel C is a bar chart showing Moco (pmol) versus 1 HBr (pmol) concentration for MPT synthesis. Panel D is a bar chart showing Moco (pmol) versus 1 (pmol) concentration for SO reconstitution. A chemical structure of ALXN1101 is also displayed.
L1884: Figure showing the percentage of animal survival (%) over time (days) for four groups of mice.
The Y-axis represents Percentage of animal survival (%), ranging from 0 to 100. The X-axis represents Time (days), ranging from 0 to 120.
The legend identifies the groups:
Placebo treated \text{MOC}S1^{+/+} mice (Solid line, top)
ALXN1101 treated \text{MOC}S1^{+/+} mice (Dotted line, top)
E. coli derived cPMP treated \text{MOC}S1^{-/-} mice (Dashed line, middle)
ALXN1101 treated \text{MOC}S1^{-/-} mice (Dotted line, bottom)
The graph illustrates that \text{MOC}S1^{+/+} mice treated with placebo or ALXN1101 maintained high survival rates (near 100%). In contrast, \text{MOC}S1^{-/-} mice treated with placebo or E. coli derived cPMP showed rapid mortality, dropping significantly within the first 20 days. ALXN1101 treatment significantly improved survival in the \text{MOC}S1^{-/-} mice, although survival was still lower than the wild-type controls.
L1885: Line graph showing Body Weight (g) versus Postnatal Day for MOC51^{-/-} mice treated with ALXN1101 or rcPMP. The main graph shows five groups: F. coli derived cPMP treated MOC51^{-/-} mice (n=12), ALXN1101 treated MOC51^{-/-} mice (n=15), Placebo treated MOC51^{-/-} mice (n=10), ALXN1101 treated MOC51^{-/-} mice (n=16), and Placebo treated MOC51^{+/+} mice (n=10). The placebo treated MOC51^{-/-} mice show a sharp decline in weight starting around postnatal day 16, reaching zero weight by day 22. The inset graph shows the mean body weight of placebo treated MOC51^{-/-} mice, confirming the rapid weight loss.
